Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-066-20260915T132641508721Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
For ice classes IA and IA Super, the rudder (the rudder stock and the upper part of the rudder) shall be protected from direct contact with intact ice by an ice knife that extends below the LIWL, if practicable (or equivalent means). Special consideration shall be given to the design of the rudder and the ice knife for ships with flap-type rudders.
Normalized requirement:
For ice classes IA and IA Super, the rudder (the rudder stock and the upper part of the rudder) shall be protected from direct contact with intact ice by an ice knife that extends below the LIWL, if practicable (or equivalent means).
Required inputs: iceClass, LIWL

CASE TRF-066-P02
Original expected verdict: PASS
Original rationale: IA Super ship uses an equivalent rudder-protection means when the ice knife is not practicable.
Source clause: 5
Test pattern: UNSPECIFIED
RDF path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/evaluation/BEHAVIORAL_RDF_R13/rdf/TRF/TRF-066/TRF-066-P02.ttl

Full unmodified RDF Turtle:
```turtle
@prefix ex: <https://w3id.org/nltl/benchmark/traficom-batch-f/TRF-066-P02/> .
```

```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:TRF066Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:datatype nltl:iceClassValue ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassIaSuper
            nltl:iceClassIa
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
            nltl:iceClassIi
            nltl:iceClassIii
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must have one valid ice class."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceKnifePracticable ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must state whether an ice knife is practicable."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gs:TRF066NotApplicableShape
        gs:TRF066IaIceKnifeShape
        gs:TRF066IaSuperIceKnifeShape
        gs:TRF066IaEquivalentShape
        gs:TRF066IaSuperEquivalentShape
    ) .

gs:TRF066NotApplicableShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
            nltl:iceClassIi
            nltl:iceClassIii
        )
    ] .

gs:TRF066IaIceKnifeShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIa ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceKnifePracticable ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasRudderProtectionArrangement ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:TRF066IceKnifeArrangementShape ;
        sh:message "For ice class IA, a practicable ice knife extending below the LIWL is required."
    ] .

gs:TRF066IaSuperIceKnifeShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIaSuper ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceKnifePracticable ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasRudderProtectionArrangement ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:TRF066IceKnifeArrangementShape ;
        sh:message "For ice class IA Super, a practicable ice knife extending below the LIWL is required."
    ] .

gs:TRF066IaEquivalentShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIa ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceKnifePracticable ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasRudderProtectionArrangement ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:TRF066EquivalentArrangementShape ;
        sh:message "For ice class IA, an equivalent rudder protection means is required when an ice knife is not practicable."
    ] .

gs:TRF066IaSuperEquivalentShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIaSuper ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceKnifePracticable ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasRudderProtectionArrangement ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:TRF066EquivalentArrangementShape ;
        sh:message "For ice class IA Super, an equivalent rudder protection means is required when an ice knife is not practicable."
    ] .

gs:TRF066IceKnifeArrangementShape

        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ice knife must extend below the lower ice waterline."
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

gen:IMO043_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasMachineryInstallation ;
        sh:class nltl:machineryInstallationItem ;
        sh:node gen:IMO043_MachineryInstallationShape ;
        sh:message "Each linked machinery installation must satisfy the applicable hazard coverage constraints."
    ] .

gen:IMO043_MachineryInstallationShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:node gen:IMO043_HazardCoverageShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasAssociatedEquipment ;
        sh:class nltl:associatedEquipmentItem ;
        sh:node gen:IMO043_AssociatedEquipmentShape ;
        sh:message "The machinery installation must identify associated equipment when such equipment is represented."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasHazardProtectionEvidence ;
        sh:class nltl:evidenceArtifact ;
        sh:message "The machinery installation must provide hazard-protection evidence when an applicable hazard is declared."
    ] .

gen:IMO043_AssociatedEquipmentShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:node gen:IMO043_HazardCoverageShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path [
            sh:inversePath nltl:hasAssociatedEquipment
        ] ;
        sh:class nltl:machineryInstallationItem ;
        sh:message "Each associated equipment item must be linked to its owning machinery installation and satisfy the applicable hazard coverage constraints itself."
    ] .

gen:IMO043_HazardCoverageShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:not [
                sh:property [
                    sh:path nltl:hasApplicableHazard ;
                    sh:hasValue nltl:iceAccretionHazard
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasHazardProtectionEvidence ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:class nltl:evidenceArtifact
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:message "Protection coverage evidence is required when ice accretion is an applicable hazard." ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:not [
                sh:property [
                    sh:path nltl:hasApplicableHazard ;
                    sh:hasValue nltl:snowAccumulationHazard
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasHazardProtectionEvidence ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:class nltl:evidenceArtifact
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:message "Protection coverage evidence is required when snow accumulation is an applicable hazard." ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:not [
                sh:property [
                    sh:path nltl:hasApplicableHazard ;
                    sh:hasValue nltl:seawaterIceIngestionHazard
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasHazardProtectionEvidence ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:class nltl:evidenceArtifact
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:message "Protection coverage evidence is required when seawater ice ingestion is an applicable hazard." ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:not [
                sh:property [
                    sh:path nltl:hasApplicableHazard ;
                    sh:hasValue nltl:liquidFreezingOrViscosityIncreaseHazard
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasHazardProtectionEvidence ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:class nltl:evidenceArtifact
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:message "Protection coverage evidence is required when liquid freezing or viscosity increase is an applicable hazard." ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:not [
                sh:property [
                    sh:path nltl:hasApplicableHazard ;
                    sh:hasValue nltl:seawaterIntakeTemperatureHazard
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasHazardProtectionEvidence ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
```

```turtle
    ] .
```

```turtle
                    nltl:seawaterIntakeTemperatureHazard
                    nltl:snowIngestionHazard
                ) ;
                sh:message "Each applicable hazard shall be one of the permitted Polar Code hazards."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasHazardProtectionEvidence ;
                sh:class nltl:evidenceArtifact ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Each machinery installation shall have hazard protection evidence."
            ]
        ]
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-001> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must have exactly one construction contract date to determine UR I2 Rev.4 applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:urI2RevisionApplicability ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must record exactly one UR I2 Rev.4 applicability value."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "UR I2 Rev.4 applicability must be true for ships contracted for construction on or after 1 January 2021 and false otherwise." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:constructionContractDate ?date ;
                      nltl:urI2RevisionApplicability ?applicability .
                FILTER (
                    (
                        (
                            YEAR(?date) > 2021 ||
                            (YEAR(?date) = 2021 && MONTH(?date) > 1) ||
                            (YEAR(?date) = 2021 && MONTH(?date) = 1 && DAY(?date) >= 1)
                        ) &&
                        ?applicability != true
                    ) ||
                    (
                        (
                            YEAR(?date) < 2021 ||
                            (YEAR(?date) = 2021 && MONTH(?date) < 1) ||
                            (YEAR(?date) = 2021 && MONTH(?date) = 1 && DAY(?date) < 1)
                        ) &&
                        ?applicability != false
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
                sh:path nltl:urI2RevisionApplicability ;

            sh:property [

                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

Clause / section / page: 6.3.3.1-.3 / Part I-A Chapter 6 - Machinery installations / 21

Original source text:
Scantlings of propeller blades, propulsion line, steering equipment and other appendages of category A ships shall be approved by the Administration, or a recognized organization accepted by it, taking into account standards acceptable to the Organization or other standards offering an equivalent level of safety; scantlings of propeller blades, propulsion line, steering equipment and other appendages of category B ships shall be approved by the Administration, or a recognized organization accepted by it, taking into account standards acceptable to the Organization or other standards offering an equivalent level of safety; and scantlings of propeller blades, propulsion line, steering equipment and other appendages of ice-strengthened category C ships shall be approved by the Administration, or a recognized organization accepted by it, taking into account acceptable standards adequate with the ice types and concentration encountered in the area of operation.
Normalized requirement:
For category A, category B, and ice-strengthened category C ships, each propeller blade, propulsion-line, steering-equipment, and appendage scantling shall have approval evidence from an accepted authority using an accepted or equivalent-safety standard; category C evidence shall address operating ice type and concentration.
Required inputs: Ship category, component inventory, scantling approval, standard, and operating-ice evidence

```turtle
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO049ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:machineryComponentType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must identify the machinery component type relevant to the scantling approval assessment."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gen:IMO049CategoryABShape
        gen:IMO049IceStrengthenedCategoryCShape
    ) .

gen:IMO049CategoryABShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:polarShipCategoryA
            nltl:polarShipCategoryB
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship category must be Category A or Category B for this approval branch."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasComponent ;
        sh:class nltl:shipComponent ;
        sh:node gen:IMO049CategoryABComponentShape
    ] .

gen:IMO049IceStrengthenedCategoryCShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:polarShipCategoryC ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship category must be Category C for the ice-strengthened Category C approval branch."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipIceStrengthened ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A Category C ship must be recorded as ice-strengthened for this approval branch."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasComponent ;
        sh:class nltl:shipComponent ;
        sh:node gen:IMO049IceStrengthenedCategoryCComponentShape
    ] .

gen:IMO049CategoryABComponentShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasApprovalRecord ;
        sh:class nltl:approvalRecord ;
        sh:node gen:IMO049CategoryABApprovalShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each applicable machinery component must have approval evidence for its scantling."
    ] .

gen:IMO049IceStrengthenedCategoryCComponentShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasApprovalRecord ;
        sh:class nltl:approvalRecord ;
        sh:node gen:IMO049IceStrengthenedCategoryCApprovalShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each applicable ice-strengthened Category C machinery component must have approval evidence for its scantling."
    ] .

gen:IMO049CategoryABApprovalShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:scantlingApprovalStatus ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The scantling approval record must have approved status."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvingAuthority ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in (
            "Administration"
            "recognized organization accepted by the Administration"
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The approval must be by the Administration or a recognized organization accepted by the Administration."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvalStandard ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in (
            "standard acceptable to the Organization"
            "another standard offering an equivalent level of safety"
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The approval must use an Organization-acceptable standard or an equivalent-safety standard."
    ] .

gen:IMO049IceStrengthenedCategoryCApprovalShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:scantlingApprovalStatus ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The scantling approval record must have approved status."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvingAuthority ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in (
            "Administration"
            "recognized organization accepted by the Administration"
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The approval must be by the Administration or a recognized organization accepted by the Administration."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvalStandard ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:hasValue "acceptable standard adequate for operating ice type and concentration" ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The approval must use an acceptable standard adequate for the operating ice type and concentration."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:operatingIceType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Ice-strengthened Category C approval evidence must state the operating ice type."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:operatingIceConcentration ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            a sh:NodeShape ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Ice-strengthened Category C approval evidence must state the operating ice concentration as a unitless quantity."
    ] .

```

```turtle
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:scantlingApprovalStatus ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:message "Each applicable scantling approval record must have approved status."
    ] .

        ) ;

    ] .
```

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 225
Batch: BATCH_23

Source family: IMO
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC

Source revision: Resolution MSC.385(94), adopted 21 Nov 2014; consolidated with MEPC.264(68), adopted 15 May 2015
Clause / section / page: 2.3.3 / Part I-A Chapter 2 - PWOM regulations / 16
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-027-20260915T103439749063Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The Manual shall include risk-based procedures for the following: voyage planning to avoid ice and/or temperatures that exceed the ship's design capabilities or limitations; arrangements for receiving forecasts of the environmental conditions; means of addressing any limitations of the hydrographic, meteorological and navigational information available; operation of equipment required under other chapters of this Code; and implementation of special measures to maintain equipment and system functionality under low temperatures, topside icing and the presence of sea ice, as applicable.
Normalized requirement:
PWOM shall include risk-based procedures covering voyage-limit avoidance, environmental forecasts, information limitations, required-equipment operation, and applicable low-temperature, topside-icing, and sea-ice functionality measures.
Required inputs: PWOM and five required procedure coverage indicators

```turtle
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF074ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceClassValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must declare an ice class to determine applicability of the propeller submersion rule."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIb
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:ballastConditionPropellerTopDepth ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:TRF074LengthQuantityShape
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:requiredPropellerSubmersionDepthHi ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:TRF074LengthQuantityShape
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIc
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:ballastConditionPropellerTopDepth ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:TRF074LengthQuantityShape
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:requiredPropellerSubmersionDepthHi ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:TRF074LengthQuantityShape
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIa
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIaSuper
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIi
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIii
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For ice class IB or IC, when the ballast-condition propeller top depth is below hi, the propulsion system must be designed for ice class IA." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass .
                FILTER (?iceClass IN (nltl:iceClassIb, nltl:iceClassIc))

                $this nltl:ballastConditionPropellerTopDepth ?depthQuantity .
                ?depthQuantity qudt:numericValue ?depth .
                ?depthQuantity qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .

                $this nltl:requiredPropellerSubmersionDepthHi ?hiQuantity .
                ?hiQuantity qudt:numericValue ?hi .
                ?hiQuantity qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .

                FILTER (
                    datatype(?depth) = xsd:decimal &&
                    datatype(?hi) = xsd:decimal &&
                    ?depth < ?hi
                )

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:propulsionSystemDesignedForIceClass nltl:iceClassIa .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:TRF074LengthQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .
```

The steering mechanism, the fitting of the unit, and the body of the thruster shall be designed to withstand the plastic bending of a blade without damage. REQUIREMENT 229: I2-021
====================================================================================================

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 229
Batch: BATCH_23
Requirement ID: I2-021
Source family: I2
Verification mode: DIRECT_CALCULATION
Source document: UR I2 Structural Requirements for Polar Class Ships
Source revision: Rev.4 Dec 2019
Clause / section / page: I2.4.1 / Shell plate requirements / 8
Source URL: https://iacs.org.uk/resolutions/unified-requirements/ur-i/ur-i2-rev4-cln
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/ur-i2rev4.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 2142453d2965997a772f451982278e622413273048097924173216bd278df9a7
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-I2-021-20260915T094149222688Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The required minimum shell plate thickness, t, is given by: t = tnet + ts [mm] where tnet = plate thickness required to resist ice loads according to I2.4.2 [mm] ts = corrosion and abrasion allowance according to I2.11 [mm] The thickness of shell plating required to resist the design ice load, tnet, depends on the orientation of the framing.
Normalized requirement:
The required minimum shell plate thickness, t, is given by: t = tnet + ts [mm] where tnet = plate thickness required to resist ice loads according to I2.4.2 [mm] ts = corrosion and abrasion allowance according to I2.11 [mm] The thickness of shell plating required to resist the design ice load, tnet, depends on the orientation of the framing.
Required inputs: thickness

Locked context, definitions, ownership, units, and dependency contract:
{
  "dependency_contract": {

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix nltlgen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

nltlgen:MillimetreQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

nltlgen:MetreQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue unit:M ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

nltlgen:PlatingThicknessShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceLoadRequiredNetPlateThickness ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node nltlgen:MillimetreQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:corrosionAbrasionAllowance ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node nltlgen:MillimetreQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:thickness ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node nltlgen:MetreQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "thickness must equal iceLoadRequiredNetPlateThickness plus corrosionAbrasionAllowance, with millimetre operands converted to metres." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceLoadRequiredNetPlateThickness ?netQuantity ;
                      nltl:corrosionAbrasionAllowance ?allowanceQuantity ;
                      nltl:thickness ?thicknessQuantity .

                ?netQuantity qudt:numericValue ?net .
                ?allowanceQuantity qudt:numericValue ?allowance .
                ?thicknessQuantity qudt:numericValue ?actual .

                BIND ((?net + ?allowance) / 1000.0 AS ?expected)

                FILTER (?actual != ?expected)
            }
        """
    ] .

nltlgen:ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPlating ;
        sh:node nltlgen:PlatingThicknessShape
    ] .

```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:I2_021_ShellPlateThicknessShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPlating ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:plating ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceLoadRequiredNetPlateThickness ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "Each plating item must provide one ice-load required net plate thickness in millimetres."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:corrosionAbrasionAllowance ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "Each plating item must provide one corrosion and abrasion allowance in millimetres."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:thickness ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "Each plating item must provide one shell plate thickness in millimetres."
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For each plating item, thickness must equal ice-load required net plate thickness plus corrosion and abrasion allowance within a relative tolerance of 1e-6 with an absolute fallback of 1e-6 mm." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasPlating ?plating .
                ?plating nltl:iceLoadRequiredNetPlateThickness ?netQuantity ;
                         nltl:corrosionAbrasionAllowance ?allowanceQuantity ;
                         nltl:thickness ?thicknessQuantity .
                ?netQuantity qudt:numericValue ?netValue .
                ?allowanceQuantity qudt:numericValue ?allowanceValue .
                ?thicknessQuantity qudt:numericValue ?thicknessValue .
                BIND((?netValue + ?allowanceValue) AS ?expectedValue)
                FILTER(
                    ABS(?thicknessValue - ?expectedValue)
                    > ((0.000001 * ABS(?expectedValue)) + 0.000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
    a sh:NodeShape ;

    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceLoadRequiredNetPlateThickness ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:corrosionAbrasionAllowance ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:thickness ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Thickness must equal the ice-load required net plate thickness plus the corrosion and abrasion allowance; values are compared after converting millimetres to metres within a relative tolerance of 1e-6 with a 1e-9 absolute fallback." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceLoadRequiredNetPlateThickness ?netQuantity ;
                      nltl:corrosionAbrasionAllowance ?allowanceQuantity ;
                      nltl:thickness ?thicknessQuantity .
                ?netQuantity qudt:numericValue ?net ;
                             qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM> .
                ?allowanceQuantity qudt:numericValue ?allowance ;
                                   qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM> .
                ?thicknessQuantity qudt:numericValue ?thickness ;
                                  qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                BIND((?net + ?allowance) / 1000 AS ?expected)
                BIND(ABS(?thickness - ?expected) AS ?difference)
                FILTER(
                    ?difference > (ABS(?expected) * 0.000001) &&
                    ?difference > 0.000000001
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

F. MANUAL REVIEW CHECKLIST — DO NOT PREFILL GRADES
Q1 Applicability: Does the shape activate for the correct ships, conditions, and scope?
Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?
Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?
Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?
Q7 Traceability / clarity: Is the implementation understandable and traceable to the cited evidence?
Record grades, reviewer identity, evidence, and notes in the workbook. Do not infer full-requirement correctness from these two cases.
